<!-- Search Menu Multi Room Script Zone -->
<script type="text/javascript">
    $(window).load(function () {
        SearchMenuMultiRoom.init();
    });
    var SearchMenuMultiRoom = {
        Engine: {
            Package: null, OnlyHotel: null
        },
        init: function () {
            SearchMenuMultiRoom.initEngineVersion();
            SearchMenuMultiRoom.SearchControl.init();
            $("#ulSearchMenuMultiRoom li").click(SearchMenuMultiRoom.liTab_Click);
            SearchMenuMultiRoom.setDefaultTab();
            $("#ddlSearchType option[style='display:none;']").remove();
        },
        initEngineVersion: function () {
            //Versiyon değişikliğinde degisecek alan
            /*[PackageSearch,SearchMenuPackageV2],[SearchMenuOnlyHotelV0,SearchMenuOnlyHotelV1,SearchMenuOnlyHotelV2]*/
            SearchMenuMultiRoom.Engine.Package = SearchMenuPackageV2;
            SearchMenuMultiRoom.Engine.OnlyHotel = SearchMenuOnlyHotelV1;
        },

        SearchControl: {
            init: function () {
                SearchMenuMultiRoom.SearchControl.InitPackageSearch();
                SearchMenuMultiRoom.SearchControl.InitOnlyHotel();
            },
            InitPackageSearch: function () {
                SearchMenuMultiRoom.Engine.Package.url = 'ScriptService/Modules/Search/HolPackSearch.asmx';
                SearchMenuMultiRoom.Engine.Package.hotelListUrl = 'sr/packagesearch';

                SearchMenuMultiRoom.Engine.Package.Prm.Default.Night = '7';

                SearchMenuMultiRoom.Engine.Package.Prm.Choose.Text = 'Izaberite';

                SearchMenuMultiRoom.Engine.Package.pageInit();

            },
            InitOnlyHotel: function () {
                SearchMenuMultiRoom.Engine.OnlyHotel.Prm.Service.url = 'ScriptService/Modules/Search/HolPackSearch.asmx';
                SearchMenuMultiRoom.Engine.OnlyHotel.Prm.Page.Url.HotelList = 'sr/packagesearch';

                SearchMenuMultiRoom.Engine.OnlyHotel.Prm.Default.Night = '7';

                SearchMenuMultiRoom.Engine.OnlyHotel.Prm.Choose.Text = 'Izaberite';

                SearchMenuMultiRoom.Engine.OnlyHotel.pageInit();

            },
            InitCultureTour: function () {
                SearchMenuCultureTours.getMonth();
            }
        },
        setDefaultTab: function () {
            var liTabs = $('#ulSearchMenuMultiRoom li.active').attr('id');
            if (Common.getQueryString('OnlyHotel') == 'True') {
                activeTab = 'liTabs0';
                if (Common.getQueryString('HolPackCat') == '4')
                    activeTab = "op3";
                else if (Common.getQueryString('HolPackCat') == '7')
                    activeTab = "op9";
            }
            else if (Common.getQueryString('IsOneWay') != '')
                activeTab = 'liTabs4';
            else if (Common.getQueryString('HolPackCat') != '')
                activeTab = 'liTabs6';
            else if (Common.getQueryString('SearchType') == 'Excursion')
                activeTab = 'liTabs1';
            else if (Common.getQueryString('SearchType') == 'Transfer')
                activeTab = 'liTabs2';

            $("#ulSearchMenuMultiRoom #" + liTabs).click();
            SearchMenuMultiRoom.LocationListOHClose(); // Tab değiştiğinde DestinationList kapanması için



        },
        liTab_Click: function () {

            var $this = $(this);
            if ($this.attr('id') == "liTabs7") { $(".SearchPanel-container").addClass("paxsearch"); } else { $(".SearchPanel-container").removeClass("paxsearch"); }
            if ($this.attr('id') == "liTabs6" && $('#ddlPeriodCT option').length==0)
                SearchMenuCultureTours.getMonth();
            var packtype = $this.data("packtype");
            var direction = $this.data("direction");
            var holpackcat = $this.data("holpackcat");
            packtype = packtype == "" ? null : packtype;
            direction = direction == "" ? null : direction;
            holpackcat = holpackcat == "" ? null : holpackcat;
            switch (packtype) {
                case 'H':
                    {
                        SearchMenuMultiRoom.Engine.Package.HolPack = { PackType: packtype, Category: holpackcat, Direction: direction }
                        SearchMenuMultiRoom.Engine.Package.pageLoad();
                        break;
                    }
                case 'O':
                    {
                        SearchMenuMultiRoom.Engine.OnlyHotel.HolPack = { PackType: packtype, Category: holpackcat, Direction: direction }
                        SearchMenuMultiRoom.Engine.OnlyHotel.pageLoad();
                        break;
                    }
                default:
                    break;
            }
            SearchMenuMultiRoom.LocationListOHClose(); // Tab değiştiğinde DestinationList kapanması için
        },
        resized: function () {// For Resize
            SearchMenuRoomArea.init();
            SearchMenuMultiRoom.init();
            SearchMenuMultiRoom.Engine.Package.pageInit();
            SearchMenuMultiRoom.Engine.OnlyHotel.pageInit();
            SearchMenuCultureTours.pageInit();
            SearchMenuExcursion.pageInit();
            SearchMenuOnlyFlight.pageInit();
        },
        LocationListOHClose: function () {
            var objLocationListOH = $("#dLocationListOH");
            objLocationListOH.removeClass("collapse");
            objLocationListOH.removeClass("collapse in");
            objLocationListOH.addClass("collapse");
        }

    }
</script>
<!-- / Search Menu Multi Room Script Zone -->

<script>
    $(window).load(function () {
        CookieConfirm.init();
        CookieConfirm.pageLoad();
    })

    var CookieConfirm = {
        Prm: {
            Cookie: {
                Name: 'cookie-confirm'
            }
        },
        init: function () {
            $('#cookie-defination-text').hide();
            $('#dCookieConfirm #btn-cookie-confirm').click(CookieConfirm.btnConfirm_Click);
            $('#dCookieConfirm #btn-cookie-detail').click(CookieConfirm.btnDetail_Click);
        },
        controller: function () {
            if (CookieConfirm.getCookie() == 1)
                CookieConfirm.toDisable();
            else
                CookieConfirm.toEnable();
        },
        toEnable: function () {
            //$('#cookie-confirm-text').modal('show');
            $('#cookie-confirm-text').show();
            $('#CookieConfirm').show();
            $('#cookie-confirm-text').show();
            $('#dCookieConfirm').removeClass('hidden');
        },
        toDisable: function () {
            $('#btn-cookie').hide();
            $('#cookie-confirm-text').hide();
            $('#cookie-confirm-text').modal('hide');
        },
        getCookie: function () {
            return CookieManager.getCookie(CookieConfirm.Prm.Cookie.Name);
        },
        setCookie: function () {
            CookieManager.setCookie(CookieConfirm.Prm.Cookie.Name, "1", 1);
        },
        btnConfirm_Click: function () {
            CookieConfirm.setCookie();
            $('#dCookieConfirm').hide();
            $('#btn-cookie-detail').show();
        },
        btnDetail_Click: function () {
            var $definationText = $('#cookie-defination-text');
            if ($definationText.css("display") == "none" || $definationText.css("visibility") == "hidden") {
                $('#cookie-defination-text').show();
            } else {
                $('#cookie-defination-text').hide();
            }
        },
        pageLoad: function () {
            CookieConfirm.controller();
        }
    }
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
                $(function () {
                    $().jetmenu();
                });
                $(document).ready(function () {
                    Common.toTooltip();
                    placingTheSearchMenuMultiRoom();
                    FootMenuHeight();
                    FooterHeightFix();
                    ListPanelHeight();

                });
                $(window).resize(function () {
                    FootMenuHeight();
                    FooterHeightFix();
                    ListPanelHeight();

                    setTimeout(function () {
                        placingTheSearchMenuMultiRoom();
                    }, 100);


                });

                function FooterHeightFix() {
                    $('#main').removeAttr("style");
                    $('.page-footer').removeAttr("style");

                    var FooterHeight = $(".page-footer").height();
                    $('#main').css({ 'padding-bottom': +FooterHeight + 'px' });
                    $('.page-footer').css({ 'margin-top': '-' + FooterHeight + 'px' });
                    $('.page-footer').css({ 'height': +FooterHeight + 'px' });

                }

                function FootMenuHeight() {
                    var enyuksek = 0;
                    var ScreenWidth = window.innerWidth;
                    if (ScreenWidth > 481) {
                        $('.FooterMenu').each(function () {
                            if ($(this).height() > enyuksek) { enyuksek = $(this).height(); }
                        });

                        $('.FooterMenu').height(enyuksek);

                    }
                    if (ScreenWidth < 481) {

                        $('.FooterMenu').removeAttr("style");

                    }
                }

                function ListPanelHeight() {
                    $('.heightcont').removeAttr("style");
                    var enyuksek = 0;
                    var ScreenWidth = window.innerWidth;
                    var isTable = $(".Table").length > 0;
                    //console.log("Panele Girdi Table kontrol edilmesi lazım");
                    if (isTable) {
                        //console.log("Table Var");
                        if (ScreenWidth > 481) {
                            //console.log("Table var 481px ten buyuk hesapla");
                             $('.heightcont').each(function () {
                                if ($(this).height() > enyuksek) { enyuksek = $(this).height(); }
                                if ($(this).closest("[data-thema='recommended']").length <= 0) {
                                    $(this).height(enyuksek)
                                }
                            });
                        }
                        if (ScreenWidth < 481) {
                            //  console.log("Table var 481px ten kucuk");
                            $('.heightcont').removeAttr("style");

                        }
                    }
                    else {
                        //console.log("Table Yok Yükseklik sil");
                        $('.heightcont').removeAttr("style");
                    }

                }

                function placingTheSearchMenuMultiRoom() {
                    if ($('#SearchMenuMultiRoomV0').length == 0)
                        return;
                    windowWidth = $(window).width();
                    var isInSearch_fix = $('.search-fix #SearchMenuMultiRoomV0').length > 0;
                    var isInMp_fix = $('.mp-fix #SearchMenuMultiRoomV0').length > 0;
                    var isMoved = false;

                    if ((windowWidth >= 749 && windowWidth < 989) && isInMp_fix == false) {
                        var Search = $('.search-area').clone();
                        $('.search-area').remove();
                        $(Search).prependTo(".mp-fix");
                        isMoved = true;
                    } else if (isInSearch_fix == false) {
                        var Search = $('.search-area').clone();
                        $('.search-area').remove();
                        $(Search).prependTo(".search-fix");
                        isMoved = true;
                    }
                    try {
                        if (isMoved)
                            SearchMenuMultiRoom.resized();
                    }
                    catch (err) { }
                }
            </script>
